Title: Beytrage zur Erlauterung der Kirchen-Reformations-Geschichten des Schweitzerlandes, heraus gegeben von Johann Conrad Fusslin; Funfter Theil
Creator: Johann Conrad Fusslin (editor); with Katharina Zell's letter to the citizens of Strasbourg and her exchange with Ludwig Rabus
Publisher: Zurich, bey Heidegger und Comp. (title page)
Publication Date: 1753 (title page, OCR line 69)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1753); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis; the host record dates the series 1741, which is not this volume's year
Language: deu
Source: archive.org item beytragezurerlau05fuss, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (775,731) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1753, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: the German black letter is fair to poor: long s, f and umlauts are often misread, but the text can be followed word by word. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: A volume of documents on the Swiss and Upper German Reformation. The editor's note on the Zell piece is at about OCR lines 905-916: he prints it from a copy headed 'Ein Brieff an die ganze Burgerschafft der Stadt Strassburg, von Catharina Zellin'. The exchange between Katharina Zell and Ludwig Rabus, superintendent at Ulm, begins at about OCR line 9555; her address to the citizens from about 9690; 'VI. Erklarung der Frau Zellin' at 13176; the piece ends at about 16345. Rabus's letters stand in the same section and are not her words. The rest of the volume was not mapped.
